- **boltzmann**: Use when reasoning about entropy as microstate-counting — Boltzmann's S = k·ln W: the entropy of a macrostate is the log of W, the number of microstates that look the same (W=1 ⇒ S=0). It is extensive (independent systems' entropies add, since ln turns product into sum) and its Gibbs form S = −k·Σ pᵢ ln pᵢ is maximised, equal to k·ln W, exactly when uniform. The microstate foundation under erpax's reciprocity-entropy; DRY collapse removes configurations so entropy falls. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/boltzmann) · ★2)
